Every billed message records `speed: standard`; `server_tool_use` zero or absent; no WebSearch or WebFetch calls. The coordinator was briefed that its usage was unmeasured; it is measurable from the session transcript and is measured here. **Harness figure vs transcript:** the Workflow tool reported 1,499,391 tokens, 94 tool uses, 200 s for the 13 enumerators. The transcripts give 10,705,084 tokens across all five classes (94 tool uses; span 20:00:00.763Z–20:03:18.847Z, 198 s). The harness scalar is not class-split and does not match the transcript sum or any class subtotal; the transcript sum is used for pricing.

## 2026-11-20T23:44-05:00__add-figure__alpoge-figures

**Record type:** Master token record consolidated from `scratch/2026-11-20-alpoge-figures/tokens.md` (working ledger retained, stamped). Token record for the figure pass on `latex/alpoge/alpoge.tex`; provenance entry under the same ID in `ai/ai-usage.md` (and the working record `scratch/2026-11-20-alpoge-figures/ai-usage.md`). Linked to task `2026-11-20T16:03-05:00__harvest-dumps__alpoge-manuscript`. Written 2026-11-20T23:44-05:00 by an accounting subagent (claude-opus-5-5, Claude Code subagent) from harness figures relayed by the coordinator. Token charges only. All rows are harness-reported per-segment totals with no class breakdown (unsplit aggregates), priced at the input rate with a cache-read-to-output bound, the ledger's convention; every cost is an estimate.

**Not additive.** Forks Q1–Q4 are forks of the coordinator and inherit its context. Each segment figure likely includes inherited context read again, and the figures within one fork may be running context sizes rather than increments. The 22 segment rows below are recorded as reported; the true marginal cost of the pass is unknown. Two readings, both labelled estimates:
- **Sum of all 22 reported figures:** 11,881,382 tokens (forks 11,162,641; reviews and fix 718,741), estimated USD 47.525528 (bound 2.376276 to 237.627640). Over-counts if the segments overlap.
- **Each fork's largest segment plus the three non-fork agents:** 4,355,591 tokens (forks 3,636,850), estimated USD 17.422364 (bound 0.871118 to 87.111820). Treats each fork's figures as running context sizes; a heuristic, not a measurement.
- Reported range: 4,355,591 to 11,881,382 tokens; estimated USD 17.422364 to 47.525528. Either may under-count billed cost, since harness aggregates in this ledger have omitted repeated cache reads.

**Cross-reference, not counted here:** the read-only figure survey (216,975 tokens, 19 tool uses, 235,393 ms; estimated USD 0.867900) is priced under task `2026-11-20T16:03-05:00__harvest-dumps__alpoge-manuscript`, amendment 5. It is not repeated in this block.

**Durations:** each row gives its own harness-measured span; the forks may have run concurrently, and start and end times were not recorded, so no union span is given and the durations are not summed.

**Unmeasured (not zero):** the coordinator (main session, claude-opus-5-5, Claude Code desktop app); the disclosure-update agent for this pass; this accounting agent. No non-token charges recorded; data scripts ran locally (Python, SageMath) at no token cost.

## 2026-10-31T01:05:39-04:00__using-leanpowers__notes-lean-rounds-6-32

**Record type:** Direct master token record for the /loop Lean formalization of `latex/notes.tex` (rounds 6–32), its coordinator session, and the two record-keeping agents. One task row with sub-rows per agent; sub-rows sum to the task row. Measured 2026-10-31T15:17:11Z from the session's JSONL transcripts; this replaces the earlier estimated block in place.

**Task subtotal (measured):** **251,995,981 tokens**, USD **84.877162**, token charges only. By class: input 2,550 · output 1,106,293 · cache write 5m 2,170,016 · cache write 1h 275,333 · cache read 248,441,789. The 27 round agents alone: 210,740,115 tokens, USD 71.735210.

**was:** estimated (unsplit harness aggregate; central = input rate) 6,732,327 tokens, USD 26.929308, bound 1.346465–134.646540, coordinator unmeasured. The harness's per-agent `subagent_tokens` figure turned out to be far below the billed total (it does not include the repeated cache reads of each turn), so the estimate understated cost by roughly 3×.

**Source and method:** `measured`. Every assistant message in each transcript carries the API `usage` object (input, output, cache-creation split into 5m and 1h, cache read). `measure_tokens.py` sums these per transcript, counting each message id once. Transcripts: coordinator `~/.claude/projects/-Users-tdupuy-repos-riemann/9b281ec9-3831-4291-a2c8-b6dca6ebfe75.jsonl`; agents `…/9b281ec9-3831-4291-a2c8-b6dca6ebfe75/subagents/agent-<id>.jsonl`. Script, CSV and the agents' reports are archived in `ai/2026-10-31-notes-lean-loop/`. Every message records `speed: standard`, so only the standard-speed valuation applies.

**Rates (claude-opus-5-5, Anthropic first-party, standard speed):** input 4.00/M · output 20.00/M · cache write 5m 5.00/M · cache write 1h 8.00/M · cache read 0.20/M (cache write 5m = 1.25× input, 1h = 2× input, cache read = 0.05× input). **Rate basis:** checked 2026-10-31, claude-api skill bundled with Claude Code 2.1.314 (model table cached 2026-10-06); no provider effective date recorded. Actual billing under the Claude Code subscription is unknown.

**Duration:** Rounds ran one at a time behind a lock file, so agent intervals do not overlap; each sub-row gives its own span. Coordinator span 2026-10-31T05:05:06.790Z – 2026-10-31T15:14:36.445Z (UTC), which includes idle cron ticks.

**Non-token charges:** none: no web search, no code-execution containers. Local `lake build` compute is unpriced.

## 2026-10-17T18:15:50-04:00__update-ai-usage__weighted-axiom-minimal-integration

**Record type:** Direct master token record for the later minimal manuscript integration and its accounting; not a rewrite or consolidation of the completed research dump.

**Captured subtotal:** **12,716,053 measured tokens** = 407,400 uncached input + 12,270,848 cache read + 37,805 output. Conditional **Standard USD 8.6610900; Fast USD 17.3221800**, token charges only. No estimated counts or assumed token-class splits. Actual account charges and complete phase usage remain unavailable, not zero.

**Boundary and exclusions:** Capture at `2026-10-17T18:17:49.884-04:00`. Root announced manuscript integration at `2026-10-17T22:10:25.123Z`. Conservatively exclude that transition response `resp_0ae00a6b6957f2f2016ad3f24c16dc819caafb55f906cae155` (usage event line 2821, 22:10:26.615Z; measured 49,791 tokens), then include only subsequent responses of the root's current turn. The first included root response is `resp_0ae00a6b6957f2f2016ad3f2533918819ca74c2ac2ab73c63f`, line 2831. Both accounting agents are restricted to their new integration-accounting turn IDs below; all earlier research-turn responses and their completion tails are excluded. Every included response ID is disjoint from all IDs already in the token master and the completed dump snapshot. This is a deliberately partial measurement of a separate phase, not an invented allocation of an inseparable total.

**Measurement:** Sum deduplicated per-response `usage` matching exact thread, turn and root-turn IDs `01a14bdd-59b0-7fe2-bfca-16a3285fb63f`. Cached input is subtracted from input to obtain uncached input; reasoning is already included in output. For root, selected sums equal its last captured cumulative turn usage minus the excluded transition response's cumulative usage. For each fresh accounting turn, selected sums equal its last captured cumulative turn usage. All counts are nonnegative, cache ≤ input, reasoning ≤ output, and total = input + output. Aggregate cache-write usage is zero, so both nonnegative retention subclasses are zero though separately unexposed.

**Rates and scope:** Official [OpenAI Enterprise rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing), checked **2026-10-17** during this task's preceding accounting pass, not an asserted effective date. Standard/Fast are conditional alternatives; actual Enterprise billing eligibility and account terms are unconfirmed. Fast doubles Standard; Codex cache writes are uncharged; Astra in Codex has no long-context multiplier. Other model requests above 272,000 input tokens use 2× input/cache and 1.5× output rates. Python 3.13.7 `Decimal` computes `sum_calls(((input-cached)*input_rate+cached*cache_rate+output*output_rate)/1e6)` per model and per-call modifier. No cross-model blended rate.

**Duration:** Union of captured intervals 442.982 seconds, not the sum of overlapping agent durations. Root's interval begins at the excluded boundary response's completion timestamp; new accounting intervals begin at their own turn contexts. These measured spans include waiting.

**Unpriced/unavailable portions:** Initial transition response, excluded research completion tails, unflushed responses, final accounting messages and later caller completion are not added. Shell/tool/session charges, discounts, regional terms, actual service mode and subscription allowance treatment are unavailable. No fresh search is recorded in this integration; the public web rate is USD 10 per 1,000 runs if applicable, and no run count or charge is invented. Standard/Fast figures are not a bill or bounds on a bill.

Conditional **Standard USD 20.3295384; Fast USD 40.6590768**, token charges only. No estimated counts or assumed class splits. Complete task cost and actual account charges are unknown.

**Coverage:** Bounded capture at `2026-10-17T18:09:44.175-04:00` of the root repair-request turn and fresh research subtree. Prior research and harvest turns are excluded by exact root-turn ID `01a14bdd-59b0-7fe2-bfca-16a3285fb63f`. Root coordination within this turn is included without invented allocation. Exact thread IDs exclude inherited response records. Later work, unflushed responses, final messages and accounting amendments are omitted, not zero. A later harvest must exclude these response IDs.

**Measurement:** Read each private rollout's complete newline-terminated prefix. Select top-level `token_usage_record` matching the root-turn and exact thread IDs; deduplicate `response_id`; sum per-response `usage`. Uncached input = input minus cached input; reasoning is included in output. Each selected turn sum matches its last captured `turn_token_usage`. Raw transcript paths are in that snapshot and remain private.

**Rates:** Checked **2026-10-17**, not an asserted effective date, at the [official OpenAI Enterprise rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing). **Arithmetic:** Python 3.13.7 `Decimal` computes each model separately: `sum_calls(((input-cached)*input_rate + cached*cache_rate + output*output_rate)/1_000_000)` with applicable per-response modifiers. No cross-model blended rate or assumed token split.

**Duration:** Captured interval union 823.638 seconds; concurrent spans merged, not added. Individual spans include waiting and end at last captured usage events.

**Other charges/modifiers:** Public web retrieval/search and shell execution occurred. Billable search-run units, tool/container/session charges, included allowance, discounts and regional terms are unavailable and excluded. Published web rate: USD 10 per 1,000 runs. Astra Ultrafast (6× Standard), regional processing (1.1×) and account terms are not assumed. Subscription allowance multipliers are not USD rates. ## 2026-10-17T16:12:36-04:00__harvest-dumps__standard-conjectures-bsd

**Task:** Survey harvest after the separately priced frontier research task. Consolidation source: `scratch/2026-10-17-standard-conjectures-bsd-harvest/tokens.md`; snapshot: `accounting-snapshot-v1.json`. The prior research snapshot’s 218 response IDs are excluded from this task.

**Captured subtotal:** **32,471,525 measured tokens** = 786,887 uncached input + 31,562,752 cache read + 121,886 output. Conditional **Standard USD 9.1051844; Fast USD 18.2103688**, token charges only. No estimated token classes or assumed splits. The snapshot is partial; final response and later calls, if any, are not priced. Actual account charges are unknown.

**Method:** `capture-accounting.py` selected current-turn per-response `token_usage_record` rows for `/root` and the four independent harvest reviewers, deduplicated by response ID, and excluded every response ID already in the frontier snapshot. Cached input is subtracted from raw input before pricing; output includes reasoning and is counted once. Aggregate cache writes were zero. Numeric response counts, per-class counters, cutoff hashes and private rollout paths are frozen in the snapshot. No response or conversation content is copied into it.

**Rates:** Checked 2026-10-17, not an asserted effective date: [official OpenAI Enterprise rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing). Standard and Fast are alternative published valuations; Fast is twice Standard. Applicability to this account is unconfirmed. ## 2026-10-17T15:50:51-04:00__frontier-dump__standard-conjectures-bsd

**Task:** Standard conjectures and BSD, including the caller's research and harvest coordination through this snapshot. Task ID minted by `update-ai-usage`; not provisional. Consolidation source: `ai/2026-10-17-standard-conjectures-bsd/tokens.md`.

**Captured subtotal:** **29,584,143 measured tokens** = 702,461 uncached input + 28,797,824 cache read + 83,858 output. Conditional **Standard USD 30.6035292; Fast USD 61.2070584**, token charges only. No estimated counts or assumed class splits. Complete task cost and actual account charges are unknown.

**Coverage:** Fixed snapshot `accounting-snapshot-v3.json`, captured `2026-10-17T16:01:42.612-04:00`. It includes caller and all exposed descendants through their individual cutoffs. Any earlier snapshot in this folder is retained as evidence but excluded from totals. Later calls, unflushed work and final responses are omitted, not valued as zero. Root work belongs to the joint human request and is not artificially apportioned between research and harvest. A subsequent harvest account must exclude these response IDs.

**Method:** `capture-accounting.py` selects top-level `token_usage_record` with root turn `01a14b69-30dc-7ad1-a6bc-d0d811d9322a` and each exact agent thread ID, deduplicates response IDs, and sums per-response `usage`. Inherited history and cumulative counters are excluded. Every selected turn's sums match its last captured `turn_token_usage`. Raw input includes cached input; uncached input is their difference. Output includes reasoning, counted once. Every cache-write aggregate is zero; both nonnegative retention subclasses therefore contribute zero, though not separately exposed.

**Rates:** Checked 2026-10-17, not an asserted effective date. See [official OpenAI rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing) and `ai/2026-10-17-standard-conjectures-bsd/pricing-evidence.md`. The per-agent blocks carry full rates. These conditional Enterprise valuations do not establish the Pro account bill. Fast doubles Standard; the two are alternatives. Decimal arithmetic prices each response at its own model's rate and applies Sol's long-context modifier when required; Astra has no Codex long-context modifier.

**Duration:** Union of captured intervals: 754.724 seconds. Concurrent spans are merged, never summed. Each agent's span appears below and can include waits.

**Other charges/modifiers:** Public web retrieval/search and local shell execution occurred. Billable search runs, tool/container/session charges, included allowance and commercial discounts are unavailable and excluded. Astra Ultrafast and regional modifiers are not assumed. These figures cover token charges only.

## 2026-10-15T15:19:20-04:00__frontier-dump__single-deninger-formalism

**Task:** Research-only package on one abstract Deninger formalism, with Flach–Morin and Connes–Consani–Marcolli as proposed realizations. Task ID minted by `update-ai-usage`, not provisional. Consolidation source: `ai/2026-10-15-single-deninger-formalism/tokens.md`.

**Captured task subtotal:** **24,180,018 measured tokens** = 810,056 uncached input + 23,268,096 cache read + 101,866 output. Conditional valuations: **Standard USD 25.7652488; Fast USD 51.5304976**, token charges only. No estimated counts or assumed class splits. The complete task total and actual account charges remain unknown.

**Coverage:** Refreshed fixed snapshot read at `2026-10-15T15:46:36.361-04:00`, near completion of the research package. Each agent ends at its exact last included usage event below. This cumulative snapshot supersedes the earlier snapshot captured at `2026-10-15T15:21:37.694-04:00`; earlier evidence is retained below and excluded from the total. Unflushed work and all later research, accounting, verification, and final messages are excluded. The omitted tail is unknown usage, not zero.

**Measurement:** Read each private rollout's complete newline-terminated prefix, then select top-level `token_usage_record` with `root_turn_id = 01a140fe-e6df-7d20-ac6c-95137a2b27c4` and the exact agent `thread_id`. Deduplicate by `response_id` and sum only per-response `usage`. Inherited ancestor records and cumulative counters are excluded. Each selected turn's sum matches its last captured `turn_token_usage`. Every count is a nonnegative integer; cached input ≤ raw input, reasoning ≤ output, and total = raw input + output. Cache-write aggregates are zero for every included response; their two nonnegative retention subclasses are therefore zero, although not individually exposed.

**Runtime evidence:** Own current-task `turn_context` records identify the caller as `gpt-6-sol`/`xhigh` and the researcher, usage owner, and token owner as `gpt-6-astra`/`max`. These are observed harness execution/configuration labels, consistent with the requests. An independent provider-response model snapshot is unavailable. Harness: Codex Desktop local collaboration, OpenAI, engine `0.162.0-alpha.17.2`, 2026-10-15. No model alias substitution is used.

**Rate basis:** Checked **2026-10-15**, not an asserted effective date, against the [official Codex rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing). Standard and Fast are alternative Enterprise scenarios; applicability to this account is unconfirmed. Fast is twice Standard. Codex cache writes have no charge; Astra has no long-context multiplier. Sol above 272,000 input tokens uses twice input/cache rates and 1.5 times output. No captured Sol request crosses that threshold.

**Arithmetic:** Python `Decimal` computes each model separately using `sum_calls(((input-cached)*input_rate + cached*cache_rate + output*output_rate)/1,000,000)`, with per-request modifiers if applicable. Raw input totals 24,078,152, including 23,268,096 cached tokens. Output includes 28,470 reasoning tokens; neither subset is counted twice.

**Wall clock:** Captured interval union: `2026-10-15T19:16:49.831000+00:00` to `2026-10-15T19:46:36.221000+00:00` (00:29:46.390). Agent intervals include waiting, including the token owner's inactive gap before this refresh, and stop at their last included usage event. Concurrent durations are merged, never summed. These are partial observed spans.

**Other charges/modifiers:** Public web retrieval/search and local shell execution occurred. Billable search runs and tool/container/session fees are unavailable and excluded. The published web rate is USD 10 per 1,000 runs. Optional Astra Ultrafast (6× Standard), regional processing (1.1×), discounts, and account terms are unresolved. Subscription allowance multipliers are not USD prices. Actual billing mode is unknown; the two valuations are neither a bill nor billing bounds.

### Retained accounting evidence

`ai/2026-10-15-adelic-positivity-prismatic-scholze/token-telemetry.json` contains response identifiers, timestamps, class counts, turn IDs, filtered runtime metadata, prefix digests, and computed valuations. `collect-token-telemetry.py` reruns the filter and arithmetic from the private rollouts; `rate-evidence.json` records the checked rates, source URLs, and applicability limits. Raw conversation text remains in the local Codex session store. The response digest uses the saved records in chronological `(timestamp, response_id)` order and compact sorted-key JSON.

Only this working ledger is consolidated in this update. Existing source ledgers already represented in the master are not counted again. Historical records, their original rate assumptions, and unavailable counts remain as recorded. This task's partial snapshot has no estimate awaiting replacement; the uncaptured completion tail remains unknown.

## 2026-10-15T12:27:02-04:00__frontier-dump__flach-morin-cyclic-assembly

**Task:** Frontier-dump investigation of four research annotations on Flach–Morin, local TP/cyclic assembly, prismatic cohomology, and Deninger/de Branges positivity. The task ID was minted by `update-ai-usage`; it is not provisional. Source for consolidation: `ai/2026-10-15-flach-morin-cyclic-assembly/tokens.md`.

**Capture and coverage:** Read at `2026-10-15T12:46:50.893-04:00`. This is a measured partial snapshot of the caller, primary researcher, provenance delegate, and token delegate. The exact last included response is stated for every agent. Later research, accounting, validation, and final-message tokens are outside this snapshot; their absence is unknown usage, not zero. No token-count estimates were used.

**Method:** Select top-level `token_usage_record` payloads with `root_turn_id = 01a14060-2c06-7562-a349-2d9b1c0ef3d5` and the exact agent `thread_id`; deduplicate by `response_id` and sum only per-response `usage`. For each included turn, that sum equals its final captured `turn_token_usage`. Do not add cumulative counters or fork-inherited ancestor records. Raw input includes cached input, so uncached input is their difference. Output already includes reasoning. Every included cache-write aggregate is zero; the nonnegative 5-minute and 1-hour subclasses are therefore both zero, though no retention subclass is separately exposed.

**Model evidence:** Model and reasoning effort come from the included agents' own `turn_context` records, not from a marketing label or a requested override. Harness: Codex desktop local collaboration, engine `0.162.0-alpha.17.2`, provider `openai`, date 2026-10-15. Exact provider-response model snapshots are not independently exposed. Requested settings and derivation provenance belong to the paired `ai-usage.md`.

**Rates and applicability:** Checked **2026-10-15** against the official [Codex token rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing), with exact IDs corroborated by [GPT-6 Sol](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/models/gpt-6-sol) and [GPT-6 Astra](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/models/gpt-6-astra). This is a check date, not a published effective date. The USD card applies to eligible Enterprise token agreements; this account's actual billing agreement is not established. Values below are conditional valuations, not billed charges. Fast is twice Standard. Codex cache writes cost zero; Astra in Codex has no long-context multiplier. Sol requests above 272,000 input tokens would use twice input/cache and 1.5 times output rates; none of the captured Sol calls reaches that threshold.

**Formula:** `USD = sum_calls((input-cached)*input_rate + cached*cache_rate + output*output_rate)/1,000,000`, applying the stated per-request modifiers. Python 3.13.7 `Decimal` arithmetic computes the figures. Standard and Fast are alternative scenarios and must not be added; they are not lower/upper bounds on the account bill. No assumed token-class split requires a bound.

**Wall clock:** The captured union runs from `2026-10-15T16:23:27.193Z` through `2026-10-15T16:46:50.627Z` (00:23:23.434). The caller interval covers the descendant intervals, so overlapping durations are not summed. Each row measures its own task-start event to last included usage record, including waits; these are partial spans, not final runtimes.

**Other charges and modifiers:** Web searches/page retrievals and local shell execution occurred. Billable search units and fees (the checked public card lists USD 10 per 1,000 web runs), container/session time charges, discounts, and actual service mode are not resolved and are excluded. Astra Ultrafast (6× Standard USD rates) and regional processing (1.1×) are named modifiers, not assumed active; neither is included. Included subscription-allowance multipliers are not USD prices.

## 2026-10-15T10:14:37-04:00__frontier-dump__algtopo-questions

**Consolidation source:** `ai/2026-10-15-algtopo-questions/tokens.md`. ID minted by `update-ai-usage`, not provisional. Snapshot read at `2026-10-15T10:48:59-04:00`; each agent's exact last included response timestamp and retained file-prefix hash appear below. The task and accounting were still running at capture, so later calls and final messages are not counted or estimated.

**Scope and method:** Select `token_usage_record` payloads with `root_turn_id = 01a13fe6-545d-7c41-89f6-0e39a67e9382` and the exact agent `thread_id`, de-duplicate by `response_id`, and sum only `usage`. Exclude the preceding categorization turn. Do not sum cumulative turn/thread counters. Each selected turn's summed calls were checked against its last cumulative `turn_token_usage`. Cached input is included in raw input, so uncached input equals input minus cached input. Reasoning is already part of output. The aggregate cache-write count is explicitly zero for every included response; it is not folded into input. Source label: **measured tokens, partial coverage; conditional published-rate valuations**.

**Rate basis:** **checked 2026-10-15**, not an asserted effective date. Official [Codex rate card](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001415-chatgpt-rate-card-enterprise-token-based-pricing), [GPT-6 Sol](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/models/gpt-6-sol), and [GPT-6 Astra](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/models/gpt-6-astra) pages were retrieved. Codex cache writes have no charge; Astra in Codex has no long-context surcharge. The Sol surcharge would be applied per request above 272,000 input tokens (twice input/cache rates and 1.5 times output); none of the included Sol calls meets that threshold. Standard and Fast are conditional scenarios; observed model/effort does not establish billing mode. Fast uses twice the Standard USD rates. Subscription allowance multipliers are not USD prices. Regional/account modifiers are unknown and not silently assumed to be charges of zero.

**Formula:** `USD = sum_calls((input-cached)*input_rate + cached*cache_rate + output*output_rate) / 1,000,000`, with the stated per-request modifiers if needed. Python 3.13.7 Decimal arithmetic produced the amounts. No split assumption or token estimate was used, so no input/output allocation bound applies; actual billing and final task cost remain unknown.

**Wall clock:** The union of captured agent spans is `2026-10-15T14:11:18.262Z` through `2026-10-15T14:48:59.249Z`. The caller span covers the others; overlapping durations are not added. Spans include waiting and, for the accounting agent, its inactive gap between turns. These are captured spans, not final task runtimes.

**Non-token charges and omitted modifiers:** Public web searches/page retrievals and local shell execution occurred. Billable search units, search/tool fees, container/session charges, regional modifiers, discounts, actual service tier, and this account's billing agreement are not recorded. None is included in these token-only valuations. Their absence from the record does not mean no charge occurred.
